By unifying Reward as an Agent with DynDiff-GRPO, we enable RL on a more reliable reward foundation with substantially diversified sampling, effectively mitigating reward hacking while yielding significant accuracy gains across multiple open-source world models, thereby demonstrating that broader exploration can scale successfully when grounded in robust verification.

</details>


### [323] [Autonomous Event-Driven Multi-Agent Orchestration for Enterprise AI at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20058)
*Harsh Rao Dhanyamraju,Leonidas Raghav,Aaron Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了DAG Plan and Execute和ReAct两种多智能体架构在208个企业级场景（Persona、Department、Enterprise三个规模级别）的表现，发现规模而非任务复杂度是影响性能的主导因素。提出的Task Manager通过优先级推理、事件合并和抢占机制，实现了连续事件监控与操作，显著降低了高优先级队列延迟（14-75%）并提升了相关事件处理正确率（超20个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI需要向连续事件监控、检测和跨专业智能体协同行动方向发展，但现有多智能体系统大多基于离散的请求-响应工作流，且在企业规模下的研究尚不充分。

Method: 在208个来自生产环境的企业场景中，分别在Persona（<10个智能体）、Department（20-80个）和Enterprise（200个）三个规模级别上评估DAG Plan and Execute和ReAct两种架构，并引入包含优先级推理、相关事件合并和抢占机制的任务管理器（Task Manager）实现连续运行。

Result: 规模而非任务复杂度主导编排性能；两种架构在小规模表现良好，但在企业规模下因智能体发现噪声成为主要瓶颈而性能下降，简单任务下降更明显。DAG Plan and Execute在小规模下精度更高、并行化更优，但企业规模下开销更大；ReAct通过增量式故障处理更具鲁棒性。Task Manager将高优先级队列延迟降低14-75%，企业规模下相关事件正确率提升超20个百分点。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统在企业规模下的性能瓶颈主要来自规模扩展带来的智能体发现噪声，而非任务复杂度；Task Manager能够有效缓解这一问题，提升连续事件处理的效率与准确性。

Abstract: Enterprise AI aims to move toward continuous event monitoring, detection, and action across specialist agents, yet existing multi-agent systems largely assume discrete request-response workflows and remain underexplored at enterprise scale. We evaluate DAG Plan and Execute and ReAct across 208 production-derived enterprise scenarios spanning Persona (<10 agents), Department (20-80), and Enterprise (200) scales, and introduce a Task Manager for continuous operation via priority inference, related-event merging, and preemption. Results show that scale, not task complexity, dominates orchestration performance: both architectures perform well at small scale but degrade at enterprise scale as agent discovery noise becomes the primary bottleneck, with simple tasks degrading more sharply than complex ones. DAG Plan and Execute offers higher precision and structured parallelization at smaller scales, but its higher overhead worsens at enterprise scale; ReAct is more robust by handling failures incrementally. The Task Manager reduces high-priority queue latency by 14-75% and improves related-event correctness by over 20 percentage points at enterprise scale.

</details>


### [324] [Process-Verified Reinforcement Learning for Theorem Proving via Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20068)
*Minsu Kim,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将Lean证明助手作为符号化过程预言机，在强化学习训练中提供稠密且可靠的策略级反馈信号，通过GRPO-style目标结合第一错误传播和第一token信用方法，在形式化推理任务上取得了优于仅结果奖励的基线效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法通常仅依赖单一的二元验证信号，而符号化证明助手能提供丰富、细粒度的结构化反馈，但这一潜力尚未被充分挖掘。本文旨在弥合结构化过程与稀疏奖励之间的差距，探索如何利用证明助手的细粒度反馈来提升形式化推理训练效果。

Method: 将证明尝试解析为策略序列，利用Lean的类型论机制标注局部正确步骤和最早失败步骤，生成稠密的、基于验证器的信用信号；将这些结构化奖励融入GRPO-style强化学习目标，采用第一错误传播和第一token信用方法，平衡结果级和过程级优势。

Result: 在STP-Lean和DeepSeek-Prover-V1.5上的实验表明，策略级监督在大多数设置下优于仅结果奖励的基线，在MiniF2F和ProofNet等基准上取得了性能提升。

Conclusion: 符号化证明助手不仅可在评估时作为验证器，还能在训练中充当过程级奖励预言机，为结合语言模型的可扩展性与符号化验证的可靠性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically has relied on a single binary verification signal, symbolic proof assistants in formal reasoning offer rich, fine-grained structured feedback. This gap between structured processes and unstructured rewards highlights the importance of feedback that is both dense and sound. In this work, we demonstrate that the Lean proof assistant itself can serve as a symbolic process oracle, supplying both outcome-level and fine-grained tactic-level verified feedback during training. Proof attempts are parsed into tactic sequences, and Lean's elaboration marks both locally sound steps and the earliest failing step, yielding dense, verifier-grounded credit signals rooted in type theory. We incorporate these structured rewards into a GRPO-style reinforcement learning objective with first-error propagation and first-token credit methods that balances outcome- and process-level advantages. Experiments with STP-Lean and DeepSeek-Prover-V1.5 show that tactic-level supervision outperforms outcome-only baselines in most settings, delivering improvements on benchmarks such as MiniF2F and ProofNet. Beyond empirical gains, our study highlights a broader perspective: symbolic proof assistants are not only verifiers at evaluation time, but can also act as process-level reward oracles during training. This opens a path toward reinforcement learning frameworks that combine the scalability of language models with the reliability of symbolic verification for formal reasoning.

</details>


### [325] [Residual-Space Evolutionary Optimization via Flow-based Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20084)
*Zhuo Cao,Lena Krieger,Fernanda Nader,Xuan Zhao,Hanno Scharr,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出残差空间进化优化框架，将基于流的生成编辑与进化算法结合，通过在残差空间分离局部利用（自花授粉）和全局探索（交叉授粉）两种搜索机制，实现在非可微/黑盒目标下的可控生成编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式数据编辑方法通常要求可微目标和基于梯度的搜索，但在基于流的生成编辑场景中（通过前向和反向积分进行编辑），目标函数往往不可微或为黑盒，现有方法无法适用。

Method: 提出残差空间进化优化框架：利用条件流匹配（CFM）将条件控制因素与实例特定残差解耦，直接在残差空间中操作；引入两种互补搜索机制——自花授粉（局部利用，通过保特征残差精炼）和交叉授粉（全局探索，通过跨异质样本重组残差）。

Result: 在MorphoMNIST反事实生成基准和晶体数据上验证，表明探索-利用分解机制能够有效平衡目标对齐、实例保留和多样性，且可泛化到真实科学领域。

Conclusion: 该框架是模型无关的，填补了基于流的生成编辑与非可微/黑盒目标之间的空白，并展示了从图像到科学数据领域的迁移能力。

Abstract: Data editing with generative methods typically requires differentiable objectives and gradient-based search. However, these assumptions break down in flow-based settings, where edits are performed through forward and backward integration and often involve non-differentiable or black-box objectives. We introduce residual-space evolutionary optimization, a model-agnostic framework that addresses this gap by combining flow-based generative editing with evolutionary algorithms. Building on the observation that conditional flow matching (CFM) can disentangle condition-controlled factors from instance-specific residuals, our framework directly operates in residual space and separates two complementary search regimes: self-pollination performs local exploitation through feature-preserving residual refinement, and cross-pollination promotes broader exploration by recombining residuals across heterogeneous samples. As a proof of concept, we validate on MorphoMNIST, a benchmark dataset for counterfactual generation, and on crystal data, demonstrating that this exploration--exploitation decomposition provides a useful mechanism for balancing target alignment, instance preservation, and diversity, and extends beyond images to real-world scientific domains.

</details>


### [326] [Multi-Head Attention-Based Feature Extractor Integration with Soft Actor-Critic for Porosity Prediction and Process Parameter Optimization in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20087)
*Kianoush Aqabakee,Leonardo Stella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合多头注意力机制与SAC算法的连续动作空间RL方法，用于增材制造工艺参数优化，在激光粉末床熔融的孔隙率预测和参数优化任务中，14个episode内收敛至322.79，优于DQN、PPO、TD3和原始SAC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散动作空间的强化学习方法在增材制造工艺优化中收敛慢、易陷入局部最优，难以满足高精度制造需求。

Method: 采用连续动作空间，将多头注意力机制与Soft Actor-Critic (SAC)算法结合，利用注意力特征提取器增强对低维输入特征中细微变化的捕捉能力，改善探索-利用平衡以避开局部极小值。

Result: 在激光粉末床熔融的孔隙率预测与工艺参数优化任务中，14个episode内收敛至322.79，收敛速度和最终奖励值均优于DQN、PPO、TD3和原始SAC，且训练稳定性更好。

Conclusion: 多头注意力增强的SAC连续动作空间方法能有效提升增材制造工艺优化的收敛效率与最终性能，为高精度制造中的RL应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Additive manufacturing process optimization requires precise parameter control to minimize defects such as porosity. Traditional reinforcement learning (RL) approaches using discrete action spaces suffer from slow convergence and susceptibility to local optima, limiting their effectiveness for high-precision manufacturing tasks. This study addresses these limitations by employing a continuous action space combined with a novel architecture that integrates a multi-head attention mechanism with the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm. The attention-based feature extractor enhances the agent's ability to capture subtle variations in low-dimensional input features, enabling more effective exploration-exploitation balance for navigating value spaces with local minima. We validate our approach on porosity prediction and process parameter optimization in laser powder bed fusion, demonstrating faster convergence and higher final reward values compared to standard RL methods including DQN, PPO, TD3, and vanilla SAC. The proposed methodology achieves a convergence value of 322.79 within 14 episodes, outperforming existing approaches while maintaining stability throughout training.

</details>


### [327] [ScaffoldAgent: Utility-Guided Dynamic Outline Optimization for Open-Ended Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20122)
*Zhibang Yang,Xinke Jiang,Yuzhen Xiao,Ruizhe Zhang,Yue Fang,XinFei Wan,Zhengxing Song,Yuxuan Liu,Yuheng Huang,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScaffoldAgent提出了一种效用引导的动态大纲优化框架，通过扩展、收缩和修订三种操作以及效用反馈机制，解决开放深度研究中大纲漂移和反馈延迟问题，在长文报告生成和事实准确性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放深度研究方法存在两个核心问题：(1) 大纲在写作前固定或仅基于局部启发式调整，随着持续的信息积累会发生大纲漂移；(2) 缺乏对大纲修改的延迟反馈评估机制，无法有效衡量修改的长期价值。

Method: ScaffoldAgent将大纲演化建模为结构化决策过程，包含三种操作：扩展(Expansion)、收缩(Contraction)和修订(Revision)。引入效用引导的反馈机制，从检索增益、结构连贯性和试生成质量三个维度估计每种大纲操作的下游价值，据此指导推理阶段的节点选择、操作调度和终止决策。

Result: 在DeepResearch Bench和DeepResearch Gym两个基准上的实验表明，ScaffoldAgent在长文报告生成和事实准确性方面一致优于现有的深度研究方法。

Conclusion: 效用引导的动态大纲优化能够有效缓解大纲漂移问题，为开放深度研究中的报告生成提供更可靠的结构化支持。

Abstract: Open-ended deep research (OEDR) requires systems to acquire knowledge through multi-round retrieval and generate coherent long-form reports. The outline plays a central role as a structural scaffold that coordinates retrieval, evidence organization, and generation. However, existing methods either fix the outline before writing or refine it with local heuristics, leading to scaffold drift under continuous information accumulation and delayed feedback for evaluating outline modifications. We propose ScaffoldAgent, a utility-guided dynamic outline optimization framework for OEDR. ScaffoldAgent models outline evolution as a structured decision process with three operations: Expansion, Contraction, and Revision, enabling controlled updates to the report scaffold. It further introduces a utility-guided feedback mechanism that estimates the downstream value of each outline operation from retrieval gain, structural coherence, and trial-generation quality. The resulting utility signal guides node selection, operation scheduling, and termination during inference. Experiments on DeepResearch Bench and DeepResearch Gym show that ScaffoldAgent consistently improves long-form report generation and factual grounding over existing deep research agents.

</details>


### [328] [RACL: Reasoning-Agent Control Layers for Continuous Metaheuristic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20142)
*Antón Asla Manzárraga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RACL（推理型智能体控制层），在元启发式算法之上部署推理智能体，通过观察、推理、假设、干预、评估、防护、策略固化和解释，动态控制优化器内部搜索行为。在车辆路径问题上，RACL在21个可行案例中全部优于或持平操作记忆策略，在18个案例中优于或持平停滞触发策略，平均成本降低0.641%，且计算开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元启发式算法（如ALNS）的搜索控制依赖人工设计的静态策略或简单触发规则，缺乏自适应推理能力，难以在运行时动态调整搜索行为以应对复杂问题实例的多样性。

Method: RACL在现有优化器之上构建推理智能体层：智能体通过观测优化器的运行内存和历史行为进行推理，形成有界假设，主动干预搜索参数，评估干预效果，应用安全护栏，将有效的控制策略固化为可重用策略，并生成可解释的决策说明。实验使用Codex作为推理智能体，通过日志解释和实时干预实现控制，后以策略代理进行可重复定量评估。

Result: 在车辆路径测试中，RACL在21个可行案例中全部优于或持平操作记忆策略（OMP），在18个案例中优于或持平停滞触发策略（STP），平均成本较STP降低0.641%。在Sevilla-9/10运行时样本中，较固定策略平均成本降低8.337%，较STP降低1.605%，且无明显计算开销。

Conclusion: RACL提供了一种通用方法，使推理智能体能够自主发现、验证、固化和解释元启发式算法的控制规则，在不改变优化器核心和业务约束的前提下提升搜索性能，具有良好的可迁移性和可解释性。

Abstract: This paper introduces RACL, a Reasoning-Agent Control Layer for metaheuristics. RACL places a reasoning agent above an existing optimizer. The agent does not replace the optimizer and does not modify business constraints. Instead, it controls the optimizer's internal search behavior by observing operational memory, reasoning over past behavior, formulating bounded hypotheses, testing interventions, evaluating outcomes, applying guardrails, consolidating useful policies and explaining its decisions. The experiment uses vehicle routing as a testbed, but the contribution is not a new routing solver, a particular ALNS configuration or a specific set of routing rules. The contribution is the RACL method: a way for a reasoning agent to discover, validate, consolidate and explain algorithmic control rules for a metaheuristic. In the current experimental setting, RACL improves or ties the Operational Memory Policy in 21 of 21 feasible cases and improves or ties a non-reasoning Stagnation-Triggered Policy in 18 of 21 feasible cases, with an average RACL vs STP cost delta of -0.641%. In the Sevilla-9/10 runtime sample, RACL improves average cost by -8.337% versus Fixed and -1.605% versus STP without showing material computational overhead. During the proof-of-concept, Codex was used as an in-the-loop reasoning agent observing executions, interpreting logs and proposing live bounded interventions. The policy proxy was later used only to make quantitative evaluation reproducible.

</details>


### [329] [BIM-Edit: Benchmarking Large Language Models for IFC-Based Building Information Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20146)
*Bharathi Kannan Nithyanantham,Clemens Kujat,Tobias Sesterhenn,Stefan Telgmann,Jörn Plönnigs,Stefan Lüdtke,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BIM-Edit基准，用于评估大语言模型在建筑信息模型（BIM）的自然语言编辑任务上的表现。该基准包含324个编辑任务，涵盖11个真实建筑模型和36个合成场景，从几何准确性、语义有效性和拓扑一致性三个维度评估。当前最佳模型平均得分仅为49.5%，且没有一个模型能完全解决超过3.4%的任务，表明LLM在结构化工程设计中的能力存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD基准大多关注从文本生成新几何模型，而忽略了工程设计中对现有模型的编辑、语义保持和关系维护等关键需求。作者旨在填补这一空白，评估LLM在结构化工程编辑任务中的实际能力。

Method: 构建BIM-Edit基准，包含324个编辑任务，涵盖11个真实IFC建筑模型和36个合成场景。任务分为直接指令、空间指令和拓扑指令三类。从几何准确性、语义有效性和拓扑一致性三个维度评估LLM输出。

Result: 最佳模型平均得分仅49.5%，没有一个模型能完全解决超过3.4%的任务。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在结构化工程设计工作流中存在显著能力差距，无法满足工程实践中对现有模型进行正确编辑、保持语义和关系的要求。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to computer-aided design (CAD) to generate design artifacts from textual instructions. In engineering practice, this requires more than creating new geometry, models must also understand existing scenes, edit them correctly, and preserve semantics and relations. However, many CAD benchmarks focus on creating new models rather than editing existing ones, and mostly evaluate geometric correctness. We introduce BIM-Edit, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on natural-language editing of Building Information Models (BIM) represented in the Industry Foundation Classes (IFC) format. BIM provides a challenging testbed because building models encode geometry together with semantic and relational structure. BIM-Edit contains 324 editing tasks spanning 11 realistic building models and 36 synthetic scenes. Tasks are expressed using three instruction categories - direct, spatial, and topological - covering both explicit and scene-grounded edits. We evaluate outputs along three dimensions: geometric accuracy, semantic validity, and topological consistency. Across evaluated LLMs, the best-performing model achieves only 49.5% average score across the three metrics, and no model fully solves more than 3.4% of tasks. These results demonstrate a substantial gap between current LLM capabilities and the requirements of structured engineering design workflows.

</details>


### [330] [Modularity-Free Conflict-Averse Training for Generalized PINNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20156)
*Heejo Kong,Beomchul Park,Sung-Jin Kim,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现过参数化PINNs存在功能模块化问题（网络自划分为任务专属模块），导致冲突避免优化失效。提出ModSync框架，通过惩罚任务专属连接、保留交互路径，防止容量驱动失败，实现SOTA精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冲突避免优化方法在模型容量增大时效果恶化，作者识别出过参数化网络的功能模块化是导致这一退化模式的根本原因，需要新的解决方案来维持跨目标交互。

Method: 提出Modular-Sparsity Synchronization (ModSync)框架，在冲突避免训练中集成结构优化，通过惩罚任务专属连接并保留促进交互的路径，实现模块稀疏性同步。

Result: 在多种PDE基准上，ModSync持续防止容量驱动失败，保持稳健的跨目标耦合，达到了最先进的精度。

Conclusion: ModSync有效解决了PINNs随容量增大而性能退化的问题，为大规模物理信息神经网络的训练提供了可靠的结构优化方案。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have become a powerful framework for solving PDEs by embedding physical laws into differentiable objectives. Despite their advances, training PINNs remains fragile: recent conflict-averse optimization schemes alleviate gradient interference between residual and boundary losses, but we show that their effectiveness deteriorates as model capacity increases. In this paper, we identify a capacity-induced failure mode, where overparameterized networks undergo functional modularity, self-partitioning into task-exclusive modules that suppress cross-objective interaction and hinder convergence toward Pareto-stationary points. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework, Modular-Sparsity Synchronization (ModSync), which integrates structural optimization into conflict-averse training by penalizing task-exclusive connections while preserving interaction-promoting pathways. Extensive experiments across diverse PDE benchmarks demonstrate that ModSync consistently prevents capacity-driven failures, sustains robust cross-objective coupling, and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy. Codes are available at \url{https://github.com/heejokong/ModSync}.

</details>


### [331] [Implicit Semantic-Aware Communication Based on Hypergraph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20162)
*Yiwei Liao,Shurui Tu,Yong Xiao,Yingyu Li,Guangming Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HISR框架，利用超图（hypergraph）表示语义知识实体间的高阶隐式关联，将实体与关系映射到专门的语义子空间，以提升语义通信的鲁棒性和推理准确性，在隐式语义解释准确率上相比现有基准提升高达36.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的语义通信方案仅能捕获成对关系，忽略了现实场景中普遍存在的群体交互、多实体关联等高阶隐式相关性，导致语义表达力不足，在噪声或信道损坏条件下语义推理易出现歧义和性能下降。

Method: 提出HISR（Hypergraph-based Implicit Semantic Reasoning）框架，采用超图建模多实体间的高阶关系，将实体及其关联映射到针对不同关系上下文定制的语义子空间中，从而解耦多样的语义交互，缓解传统图嵌入方法中的过度平滑问题，并在传输部分信息丢失时仍能进行鲁棒的语义推理。

Result: 数值结果表明，HISR在隐式语义解释准确率上相比最先进的基准方法提升了高达36.6%。

Conclusion: 超图结构能够更有效地建模高阶语义关联，通过语义子空间解耦设计可显著提升语义通信系统的推理准确率和抗干扰鲁棒性。

Abstract: Semantic-aware communication has emerged as a transformative paradigm for next-generation communication systems, shifting the fundamental goal from transmitting bit-level symbols to reliably recovering and understanding the semantic meaning of information. Previous studies have demonstrated that representing the semantic content of source messages as graph-based structures can significantly improve communication efficiency and the accuracy of semantic inference at the receiver. However, existing solutions typically employ graphs that capture only pairwise relationships, thereby neglecting higher-order implicit correlations commonly observed in real-world scenarios, such as group interactions, multi-entity associations, and complex relational contexts. This limitation reduces semantic expressiveness and makes semantic inference susceptible to ambiguity and performance degradation, particularly under noisy or corrupted channel conditions. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel hypergraph-based implicit semantic reasoning framework, HISR, which leverages hypergraphs to represent complex multi-entity relationships among semantic knowledge entities. In HISR, entities and their associated higher-order relations are mapped into dedicated semantic subspaces tailored to distinct relational contexts. This design not only disentangles diverse semantic interactions to mitigate the over-smoothing effects commonly found in traditional graph embedding methods but also enables robust semantic inference even when partial information loss occurs during transmission. Numerical results show that the proposed HISR achieves up to a 36.6% improvement in implicit semantic interpretation accuracy over the state-of-the-art benchmarks.

</details>


### [332] [Beyond Accuracy: Measuring Logical Compliance of Predictive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20208)
*Guillaume Olivier Delplanque,Pierre Genevès,Nabil Layaïda,Zephirin Faure*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出规则违反分数（RVS），一种独立于预测精度、用于量化模型输出是否符合逻辑规则的补充评估指标，可自动从Horn规则生成SQL查询计算，并在三大基准上验证了其能揭示标准指标无法捕捉的模型行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标仅关注预测精度（如排名质量、分类准确率等），无法衡量模型输出是否满足预定义的逻辑或领域特定约束，而在医疗、金融、自动驾驶等高危应用中，逻辑一致性与预测精度同等重要。

Method: 提出RVS指标，区分硬规则（严格约束）与软规则（统计规律），支持任何关系词汇表上的预测模型，并通过自动生成SQL查询计算Horn规则的违反情况。

Result: 在知识图谱链接预测与关系回归三大基准上，对比了基于规则、基于嵌入及神经符号模型，发现预测精度相近的两个模型在逻辑合规性上可能存在显著差异。

Conclusion: RVS作为一种通用、自动化的逻辑一致性度量，能有效补充传统预测性能指标，揭示模型行为中标准指标无法体现的差异，并可用于评估训练数据集的逻辑一致性与规则定义质量。

Abstract: Machine learning models are predominantly evaluated through predictive performance metrics such as ranking quality, prediction error, or classification accuracy. While these metrics effectively quantify how closely predictions match the ground truth, they do not assess whether model outputs respect predefined logical or domain-specific constraints. In high-stakes applications, including healthcare, finance, and autonomous systems, logical consistency can be as critical as predictive accuracy, yet no standard metric captures this dimension. We introduce the Rule Violation Score (RVS), a complementary evaluation metric that quantifies the extent to which a predictive model respects a given set of logical rules, independently of predictive accuracy. RVS treats hard rules (strict constraints) and soft rules (statistical regularities) differently, can be evaluated on any dataset and on any predictive model expressed over a relational vocabulary, and can be computed using SQL queries that are automatically generated for Horn rules. Beyond evaluating models, RVS can also evaluate the logical consistency of training datasets and help identify poorly defined rules. We evaluate RVS on three benchmarks covering knowledge graph link prediction and relational regression, including rule-based, embedding-based, and neuro-symbolic predictive models. Our results demonstrate that two models achieving comparable predictive accuracy can exhibit substantially different levels of logical compliance, revealing differences in model behavior that standard metrics fail to capture.

</details>


### [333] [Augmenting Game AI with Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20210)
*Alessandro Sestini,Joakim Bergdahl,Amir Baghi,Jean-Philippe Barrette-LaPierre,Florian Fuchs,Linus Gisslén*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了强化学习在游戏AI中的应用潜力，提出了一个面向游戏AI和游戏开发的强化学习训练框架，并通过实例展示了强化学习增强的游戏AI，同时指出了当前研究瓶颈和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手工编码的游戏AI难以实现复杂行为，导致玩家沉浸感受到破坏。引入机器学习（尤其是强化学习）可以创建更真实、可信的游戏角色，但目前研究局限性阻碍了其在各类游戏中的广泛部署。

Method: 提出一个面向游戏AI和游戏开发的强化学习训练框架，该框架包含一系列针对游戏AI的需求约束；通过实例展示强化学习增强的游戏AI；分析实际部署中的实际问题。

Result: 给出了强化学习增强游戏AI的示例，识别了当前部署中的瓶颈和难点问题，指出了加速机器学习在游戏AI中应用的研究方向。

Conclusion: 强化学习在游戏AI领域具有广阔前景，但需要克服当前研究限制才能实现跨游戏类型的广泛部署；本文提出的框架和识别的研究方向有助于推动这一进程。

Abstract: Immersion in video games depends not only on graphics, audio, and game mechanics, but also on the quality of in-game characters. Producing believable characters, or game AI, remains a significant challenge as behavioral complexity is hard to capture with hand-coded systems. Game AI is a source of immersion and engagement; however, the limitations stemming from the challenges of creating game AI often lead to frustration and the breaking of the illusion of realism within the game. The introduction of machine learning models opens the door to creating more believable, authentic, and relatable characters in games. The promise is that they either learn from interacting with the game, or from player data, to develop true human-like behavior. In this paper, we envision more applications of reinforcement learning for game AI in the future. For this to materialize, current research limitations are prohibitive to broad deployment across game genres. Therefore, we propose a framework for training reinforcement learning models with a set of requirements in mind that are suited towards game AI and game development. We present examples of games with reinforcement learning-augmented game AI and describe the practicalities of deploying player-facing machine learning agents in modern games. Furthermore, we identify bottlenecks and hard problems in these areas, which we believe offer promising research directions to accelerate the adoption of machine learning in game AI for the video game industry.

</details>


### [334] [QMFOL: Benchmarking Large Language Model Reasoning via Quantifiable Monadic First-Order Logic Test Case Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20227)
*Xinyi Zheng,Ling Shi,Tianlong Yu,Yongxin Zhao,Lorenz Goette,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QMFOL框架，用于生成具有可控复杂性的一阶逻辑推理任务，并构建了包含2880个实例的基准测试QMFOLBench。评估显示，随着逻辑复杂度增加，模型性能下降而计算开销上升；模型在True标签任务上表现优于False或Unknown任务，且对语义变化敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理基准缺乏对逻辑复杂度的细粒度控制，难以在语义多样性与逻辑一致性之间取得平衡，无法跟上LLM推理能力的提升速度。

Method: 提出QMFOL自动化框架，使用合取与析取模式构建形式逻辑结构，精确控制推理深度、宽度、标签类型和干扰项；通过LLM将形式结构翻译为自然语言，并利用外部证明器进行往返验证确保逻辑一致性。

Result: 构建了QMFOLBench基准（2880实例，960种配置）；在6个大型推理模型和2个LLM上的评估表明：逻辑复杂度增加导致性能下降和计算开销上升；模型在True标签任务上表现更好；模型对语义变化敏感。

Conclusion: QMFOL为构建具有可控复杂度的演绎推理基准提供了可扩展且可靠的方法，能够更精确地评估现代语言模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made significant progress in reasoning, particularly in deductive reasoning, which is crucial for high-stakes decision-making. As models improve, evaluation benchmarks should evolve to keep pace. However, existing benchmarks lack fine-grained control over logical complexity and struggle to balance semantic diversity with logical consistency.
  To address these issues, we propose QMFOL, an automated framework for generating monadic first-order logic reasoning tasks with quantifiable and controllable complexity. It constructs formal logical structures using conjunction and disjunction patterns, enabling precise control over reasoning depth, width, label types, and distractors. These structures are then translated into natural language via LLMs, with logical consistency ensured through round-trip verification using an external prover. Based on our framework, we build QMFOLBench, a benchmark comprising 2880 instances with 960 configurations across diverse logical and semantic dimensions. Evaluations on six large reasoning models (LRMs) and two LLMs show that performance degrades and computational overhead increases with rising logical complexity. Models perform better on True-labeled tasks than on False or Unknown ones, and exhibit sensitivity to semantic variation. Overall, QMFOL offers a scalable and reliable approach for constructing deductive reasoning benchmarks with controllable complexity, enabling more precise evaluation of reasoning capabilities in modern language models.

</details>


### [335] [Thermodynamic Measure of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20231)
*Ishanu Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出智能可被定义为对稀有但有效未来的合法放大，并通过递归自我模拟架构建立了智能的热力学度量框架，证明了该架构对高"热力学智能"的必要性和近似充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对智能的理解缺乏统一的、可量化的定义。作者试图从第一性原理出发，建立一种普适的、可在热力学尺度上度量的智能理论，涵盖从被动物质到人类智能的广泛范围。

Method: 从智能系统必须建模世界及其自身位置这一前提出发，推导出递归自我模拟的必要性。通过热力学度量"稀有-有效提升"（rare-valid lift）来量化智能，并证明该度量与递归自我模拟架构之间的理论关系。

Result: 给出了一个必要性陈述和一个条件性的近充分性陈述：高稀有-有效提升要求内部模拟对稀有-有效未来具有高保真度；反之，当保真度高且模拟包含有效策略时，可实现的提升接近驱动限制的最优值。

Conclusion: 递归自我模拟不仅是智能的一个合理特征，在给定假设下，它对高热力学智能是必要且几乎充分的。该框架使智能能够在统一尺度上被测量，从被动物质到人类。

Abstract: Can intelligence be measured? We propose that intelligence can be defined as the lawful amplification of rare but valid futures: a system increases the probability of outcomes that would be unlikely under passive dynamics but remain admissible under the constraints of the domain. We start with the premise that an intelligent system must model the world and its own place within it. Because the system is part of the world it models, this leads naturally to recursive self-simulation: the system represents futures in which its own actions are part of the trajectory. Our central results give a necessity statement and a conditional near-sufficiency statement connecting this architecture to a precise thermodynamic measure of lawful amplification of rare-valid futures: high rare-valid lift is impossible unless the internal simulation identifies rare-valid futures with high fidelity; conversely, when rare-valid fidelity is high and the simulation contains an effective policy, the achievable lift approaches the actuation-limited optimum. Thus recursive self-simulation is not merely a plausible feature of intelligence but, under the stated assumptions, is necessary and nearly sufficient for high thermodynamic intelligence. The resulting framework makes intelligence measurable on a universal scale, from passive matter and feedback controllers, large language models, and humans as text generators to Maxwell-demon-like information engines.

</details>


### [336] [A Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20236)
*Federica Filippini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAMO提出了一种多智能体强化学习方法，通过将奖励权重的选择本身建模为学习问题，从而在动态环境中自动平衡成本最小化与约束违反避免之间的权衡，无需手动调整权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于拉格朗日思想的RL方法将成本和约束违规合并为单一标量奖励，但奖励权重的选择依赖人工调参，在非平稳环境中难以找到合适的权衡。

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习（MAMO），将任务执行与目标设计解耦，将奖励权重的选择形式化为一个学习问题。

Result: 实现了更自主、鲁棒的RL解决方案，能够在动态环境中自动平衡优化目标与约束满足。

Conclusion: MAMO为动态环境下的约束优化问题提供了一种无需手动调参的自动化权衡方法，是迈向更自主RL方案的第一步。

Abstract: Many decision-making problems in computing and networking systems can be naturally formulated as cost-minimization problems under performance constraints. In dynamic environments, reinforcement learning (RL) is often used to solve such problems at runtime by embedding both costs and constraint violations into a single scalar reward through weighted penalty terms, following a Lagrangian-inspired formulation. However, in this context the behavior of the learned policy critically depends on the choice of these weights, which are typically selected manually. This makes it difficult to identify an appropriate trade-off between optimizing the primary objective and effectively avoiding constraint violations, particularly in non-stationary environments where their relative importance may change. This paper presents MAMO (Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization), an approach to tackle this balancing problem through multi-agent RL. MAMO decouples task execution from objective design by formulating the selection of reward weights as a learning problem, providing a !rst step towards more autonomous and robust RL-based solutions for constrained optimization problems in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [337] [Navigating Unreliable Parametric and Contextual Knowledge: Explicit Knowledge Conflict Resolution for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20245)
*Huang Peng,Jiuyang Tang,Weixin Zeng,Hao Xu,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MACR框架，通过多智能体推理和自适应知识评估，解决大语言模型中内外知识冲突问题，超越了传统二选一的冲突规避范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理LLM知识冲突时，要么信任模型内部参数知识，要么信任外部上下文信息，忽略了双方都可能存在错误，且只是规避冲突而非主动解决冲突。

Method: 1) 自适应知识评估与检索：用改进的语义熵量化模型置信度，据此决定外化内部知识或检索外部知识；2) 归纳式多智能体推理框架：三个专用智能体分别负责归纳显式规则、分析潜在冲突、解决所有上下文间的不一致。

Result: 在多个基准上显著优于现有最优基线，并能提供可解释的冲突解决方案。

Conclusion: MACR通过主动解决知识冲突而非简单规避，有效提升了LLM在混合知识源场景下的推理能力与可解释性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across a wide range of language-based tasks by leveraging both extensive parametric knowledge and in-context learning ability, enabling them to incorporate external information provided in the input prompt. However, the integration of external knowledge can introduce conflicts, not only between the model's internal parametric knowledge and the external information, but also among multiple pieces of external contexts. Existing approaches typically assume that either the model or the provided context is reliable, overlooking the possibility that both sources may contain errors, and avoid conflicts by privileging one source over the other, rather than actively resolving inconsistencies. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework MACR for LLM knowledge conflict resolution that moves beyond the conventional binary choice paradigm and incorporates an explicit conflict-resolution mechanism based on a multi-agent reasoning approach. Specifically, we first propose an adaptive knowledge assessment and retrieval approach that employs a modified semantic entropy measure to quantify an LLM's confidence in its answer to a given query. Based on this confidence estimation, MACR either externalizes the model's internal knowledge as textual representations or retrieves relevant external knowledge when internal knowledge is insufficient, generating basic contexts for subsequent reasoning. Then we introduce an inductive multi-agent reasoning framework with three specialized agents that, respectively, induce explicit rules, analyze potential conflicts, and resolve inconsistencies across all available contexts. Empirical results demonstrate that MACR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across benchmarks, while also providing interpretable resolutions of explicit conflicts.

</details>


### [338] [Confidence-Aware Automated Assessment of Student-Drawn Scientific Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20264)
*Luyang Fang,Yingchuan Zhang,Jongchan Park,Zhaoji Wang,Ping Ma,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于视觉模型的学生科学绘画自动评分方法，采用参数高效适配的Vision Transformer，并提出置信度感知评分框架，通过测试时预测分布推导响应级置信度，实现高置信度响应自动评分、不确定案例交由人工审核的选择性自动化，在六个NGSS初中评估项上提升了评分可靠性并支持自动化覆盖与评分风险的实用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生生成的绘画在科学教育中被广泛用于评估概念理解，但人工评分成本高、难以大规模实施，亟需自动化评分方法以降低评估成本并提升可持续性。

Method: 采用Vision Transformer (ViT)并进行参数高效适配，构建置信度感知评分框架，从测试时预测分布中提取响应级置信度信号，据此实现选择性自动化：高置信度样本自动评分，低置信度样本交由人工审核。

Result: 在六个NGSS初中评估项上的实验表明，该方法提升了评分可靠性，并在自动化覆盖率与评分风险之间实现了有效权衡。

Conclusion: 置信度感知方法在可信赖的教育评估中具有重要价值，为实现自动化评分与人工审核的有机结合提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Student-generated drawings are widely used in science education to assess learners' conceptual understanding in modeling-based tasks aligned with the Next Generation Science Standards (NGSS). However, scoring such drawings requires expert human judgment to interpret complex visual representations, making large-scale assessment costly to implement and sustain in classroom settings. In this work, we study automated scoring of student-generated scientific drawings using a vision-based model. We evaluate a Vision Transformer (ViT) with parameter-efficient adaptation and propose a confidence-aware scoring framework that derives response-level confidence from test-time predictive distributions. This confidence signal enables selective automation by scoring high-confidence responses automatically while deferring uncertain cases for human review. Experiments on six NGSS-aligned middle school assessment items show that the proposed approach improves scoring reliability while supporting a practical trade-off between automated coverage and scoring risk, highlighting the value of confidence-aware methods for trustworthy educational assessment.

</details>


### [339] [Lagrange: An Open-Vocabulary, Energy-Based Sparse Framework for Generalized End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20274)
*Shihao Ji,HongXi Li,Zihui Song,Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Lagrange框架，通过掩码潜在场（MLF）结合视觉语言模型（VLM）的开放词汇推理与拉格朗日动作最小化，实现稀疏、可解释且运动学可行的开放世界自动驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法在表征效率与泛化能力之间存在矛盾：稠密模型（如占据网络）几何鲁棒但计算昂贵且缺乏高层语义推理；稀疏查询规划器高效但依赖闭集定义，对分布外（OOD）事件脆弱；VLA模型虽能开放词汇推理，但其自回归离散令牌生成与连续高频控制需求冲突。

Method: 提出Lagrange框架，核心为掩码潜在场（MLF）：利用VLM编码类别无关的目标提议为连续语义视觉令牌；引入意图驱动的掩码交叉注意力模块，时序过滤无关实体；将注意力令牌解码为空间坐标上的隐式连续能量场；将决策建模为在该能量场上的拉格朗日动作最小化问题，同时满足车辆运动学约束与碰撞避免。

Result: 在nuScenes（标准场景）和CODA（长尾场景）基准上的离线评估表明，Lagrange建立了稳健、可解释且运动学可行的开放世界自动驾驶框架。

Conclusion: Lagrange通过融合VLM的开放语义理解与基于能量场的运动学优化，有效弥合了稀疏表征效率与开放世界泛化之间的鸿沟，为自动驾驶提供了一条有前景的新范式。

Abstract: Scaling end-to-end autonomous driving to complex, open-world environments requires perceptual models that generalize to anomalous scenarios and planners that produce kinematically valid trajectories. Existing paradigms face a distinct dichotomy between representational efficiency and generalization capacity. Dense models (e.g., occupancy networks), while geometrically robust, incur critical computational bottlenecks and struggle with high-level semantic reasoning. Conversely, sparse, query-based planners are efficient but reliant on closed-set definitions, rendering them vulnerable to out-of-distribution (OOD) events. Although recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer open-vocabulary reasoning, their autoregressive, discrete token generation fundamentally conflicts with the continuous, high-frequency control requirements of vehicle dynamics. To address this, we propose Lagrange, an open-vocabulary, computationally sparse driving framework based on Masked Latent Fields (MLF). Rather than relying on dense volumetric reconstructions or closed-set query mechanisms, Lagrange exploits Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to encode class-agnostic object proposals into continuous semantic visual tokens. We introduce an intent-driven masked cross-attention module that temporally filters irrelevant entities, decoding the attended tokens into an implicit continuous energy field defined over spatial coordinates. By framing decision-making as a Lagrangian action minimization problem spanning this energy field, we enforce strict compliance with vehicle kinematics while executing collision avoidance. Extensive offline evaluations on both standard (nuScenes) and long-tail (CODA) benchmarks demonstrate that Lagrange establishes a promising framework for robust, interpretable, and kinematically feasible open-world autonomy.

</details>


### [340] [Leveraging systems' non-linearity to tackle the scarcity of data in the design of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20323)
*Giancarlo Santamato,Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Massimiliano Solazzi,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度迁移学习的振动智能故障诊断方法，通过周期性多激励水平程序利用系统非线性特性生成图像，并引入新的数据可视化和增强技术，在强数据稀缺条件下实现有效诊断，经铁路受电弓结构实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度迁移学习虽能高效构建智能故障诊断系统，但仍严重依赖大量标注数据，而在机器或结构故障场景中获取充足数据极具挑战性。

Method: 采用周期性多激励水平程序，利用真实系统的固有非线性特性生成可被预训练CNN便捷分析的图像；提出新的数据可视化方法及其增强技术，以应对数据稀缺问题。

Result: 在铁路受电弓结构上的实验验证为所提方法提供了有效支持。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在强数据稀缺条件下有效构建基于振动信号的智能故障诊断系统，减轻了对大量标注数据的依赖。

Abstract: Deep Transfer Learning (DTL) allows for the efficient building of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems (IFDS). On the other hand, DTL methods still heavily rely on large amounts of labelled data. Obtaining such an amount of data can be challenging when dealing with machines or structures faults. This document proposes a novel approach to the design of vibration-based IFDS using DTL in condition of strong data scarcity. A periodic multi-excitation level procedure leveraging intrinsic non-linearities of real-world systems is used to produce images that can be conveniently analysed by pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) to diagnose faults. A new data visualization method and its augmentation technique are proposed in this paper to tackle the typical lack of data encountered during the design of IFDS. Experimental validation on a railway pantograph structure provides effective support for the proposed method.

</details>


### [341] [SoftSkill: Behavioral Compression for Contextual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20333)
*Xijia Tao,Yihua Teng,Xinyu Fu,Ziru Liu,Kecheng Chen,Yuzhi Zhao,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftSkill方法，将自然语言技能文件压缩为轻量级连续上下文向量（软技能），通过可训练的软增量微调，在保持基础模型冻结的同时实现行为控制。在多个任务上，32长度的软技能前缀显著优于无技能提示和传统的SkillOpt方法，验证了将技能作为隐式潜在控制而非显式Markdown文本的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体技能通常以自然语言Markdown文件形式部署，虽然可读性强且可移植，但每次任务推理时，冻结的语言模型都需要将冗长的文本工件重新解释为生成行为，这种间接消费方式效率低下。本文旨在探索能否将自然语言技能直接初始化为紧凑的连续上下文对象，并通过可训练的软增量进行精炼，从而让冻结的基础模型更高效地"进入"任务状态。

Method: 提出SoftSkill方法：首先将自然语言技能文件初始化为紧凑的连续上下文向量（软技能），然后通过下一个令牌预测任务对软增量进行训练，同时保持基础模型参数完全冻结。推理时，这些软技能作为隐式行为先验（latent behavioral priors）以prefix形式注入模型。在更困难的智能体执行场景中，还探索了稀疏轨迹模仿作为训练信号。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B模型上，32长度的SoftSkill前缀相比无技能提示在SearchQA上提升8.3点，LiveMath上提升42.1点，DocVQA上提升1.3点。相比SkillOpt方法，SoftSkill在SearchQA上准确率提升5.2点，LiveMath上提升12.5点，同时将数百到数千个Markdown技能令牌替换为少量虚拟令牌。

Conclusion: 一些任务技能更适合被处理为紧凑的隐式潜在控制（latent controls），而非需要在推理时重新解释的额外Markdown文本。SoftSkill展示了这种新范式的有效性，但在压缩长程过程性行为方面仍有挑战，特别是在复杂的智能体执行场景中。

Abstract: Agent skills are commonly deployed as natural-language Markdown files that encode answer policies, evidence-use habits, and task procedures. These files are readable and portable, but they are consumed indirectly: for each task instance, a frozen language model must translate a long textual artifact into generation-time behavior. This paper asks whether a natural-language skill can instead initialize a compact continuous context object, refined by a trainable soft delta while the base model remains frozen. We propose SoftSkill, a frozen-backbone method that tunes such soft skills with next-token prediction and deploys them as latent behavioral priors at inference time. In our main single-round setting, a length-32 SoftSkill prefix on Qwen3.5-4B improves over no-skill prompting by 8.3 points on SearchQA, 42.1 points on LiveMath, and 1.3 points on DocVQA. Relative to SkillOpt, SoftSkill improves accuracy by 5.2 points on SearchQA and 12.5 points on LiveMath, while replacing hundreds to thousands of Markdown skill tokens with a few virtual tokens. We further study agentic execution as a harder boundary case, where sparse trajectory imitation provides useful signal but does not yet robustly compress long-horizon procedural behavior. More broadly, the results suggest that some task skills are better treated not as additional Markdown to be reinterpreted at inference time, but as compact latent controls over how a frozen model enters the task.

</details>


### [342] [Automating SKILL.md Generation for Computer-Using Agents via Interaction Trajectory Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20363)
*Yuexing Hao,Xiaomin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个三阶段流水线从GUI交互轨迹中挖掘可读的技能库，发现虽然聚类结果具有高可读性（8个聚类中5个纯净度≥0.95），但技能感知策略在IW技能步骤准确率上仅从18.5%提升至20.5%，对BrowseComp+几乎无改善，且低于简单频率先验，表明轨迹挖掘可暴露可检查的技能结构，但当前方法不足以实现可靠的跨域策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究能否从交互数据中挖掘显式的技能库，并使其有效提升下游策略性能。

Method: 三阶段流水线：分割GUI轨迹→将片段聚类为候选技能→基于注释训练技能感知策略。使用GRPO优化，评估InteraSkill Workflows和BrowseComp+基准。

Result: 5/8聚类纯净度≥0.95；IW技能步骤准确率从18.5%略升至20.5%；BrowseComp+几乎无变化；关键源域指标低于频率先验。

Conclusion: 轨迹挖掘能暴露可检查的技能结构，但当前边界检测器、无序片段表示和离线奖励模型不足以实现可靠的跨域策略改进。

Abstract: Explicit skill libraries make computer-using agents easier to inspect, but it remains unclear whether such libraries can be mined from interaction data in a way that improves downstream policies. We study this question through a three-stage pipeline that segments GUI trajectories, clusters segments into candidate skills, and trains a skill-aware policy from the resulting annotations. The mined clusters are readable on the source benchmark: five of eight clusters have at least 0.95 purity against InteraSkill Workflows labels. However, readability does not imply transfer. GRPO improves IW skill-step accuracy only from 18.5\% to 20.5\%, leaves BrowseComp+ essentially unchanged, and underperforms trivial frequency priors on key source-domain metrics. We therefore present the method as a diagnostic study: trajectory mining can expose inspectable skill structure, but the current boundary detector, orderless segment representation, and offline reward model are insufficient for reliable cross-domain policy improvement.

</details>


### [343] [Rethinking Shrinkage Bias in LLM FP4 Pretraining: Geometric Origin, Systemic Impact, and UFP4 Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20381)
*Qian Zhao,Kunlong Chen,Changxin Tian,Zhonghui Jiang,Haitao Zhang,Chaofan Yu,Peijie Jiang,Mingliang Gong,Jia Liu,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现E2M1等非均匀4位格式存在由几何不对称性导致的收缩偏差（Shrinkage Bias），该偏差会随层数累积并被随机Hadamard变换放大，导致训练不稳定；相反，均匀网格（E1M2/INT4）可避免此问题。作者提出UFP4，一种统一4位训练方案，在1.5B稠密、7.9B MoE和124B MoE模型上均优于E2M1基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FP4训练（如NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin、AMD MI350）依赖E2M1非均匀格式，但其训练不稳定的根本原因尚未被统一解释。本文旨在揭示这一问题的根源并提出更优方案。

Method: 理论分析表明非均匀格式存在收缩偏差（Shrinkage Bias）；提出UFP4：对三种训练GEMM均应用RHT，仅对dY使用随机舍入，采用E1M2/INT4均匀4位网格。

Result: 在1.5B稠密、7.9B MoE和124B MoE长程预训练中，UFP4在BF16相对损失退化方面一致优于强E2M1基线，并通过规模定律分析和消融实验验证。

Conclusion: 未来加速器应支持E1M2/INT4风格均匀4位网格作为与E2M1并列的一等训练原语。

Abstract: FP4 training promises substantial reductions in memory and computation cost for LLM pretraining, yet current FP4 hardware paths and recipes, including NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin-class systems and AMD MI350-series GPUs, remain centered on E2M1 data elements. In this study, we identify a fundamental limitation of that choice: non-uniform formats such as E2M1 inherently suffer from Shrinkage Bias, a systematic negative rounding error caused by the geometric asymmetry of their representable bins. We show that this bias accumulates multiplicatively across layers and is amplified by the Random Hadamard Transform (RHT), providing a unified explanation for the training instability observed in existing E2M1-based FP4 recipes. In contrast, uniform grids (E1M2/INT4) bypass this grid-geometry error and better convert the improved bucket utilization from RHT into higher quantization quality. Based on this finding, we propose UFP4, a uniform 4-bit training recipe that applies RHT to all three training GEMMs while restricting stochastic rounding to dY alone. On Dense 1.5B, MoE 7.9B, and MoE 124B long-run pretraining, UFP4 consistently achieves lower BF16-relative loss degradation than strong E2M1-based baselines, supported by scaling-law analysis and ablation studies. Our results suggest that future accelerators should support E1M2/INT4-style uniform 4-bit grids as first-class training primitives alongside E2M1.

</details>


### [344] [Interpretable Sperm Morphology Classification via Attention-Guided Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20438)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Thomas Ebner,Lars Johansson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种注意力引导的深度学习框架用于精子形态分类，结合EfficientNet-B0和CBAM模块，在SMIDS和HuSHem数据集上分别达到90.2%和93.9%的准确率，并通过Grad-CAM++提供可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 男性不育常与异常精子形态相关，现有深度学习模型缺乏可解释性，限制了临床采用。因此需要一种既准确又可解释的精子形态分类方法。

Method: 采用预训练的EfficientNet-B0作为骨干网络，结合卷积块注意力模块(CBAM)聚焦精子头部关键区域，并使用Grad-CAM++可视化决策依据。

Result: 在SMIDS数据集上准确率90.2%（宏F1=0.913），在HuSHem数据集上准确率93.9%（宏F1=0.948），优于SimpleCNN和标准EfficientNet-B0。

Conclusion: 该准确且透明的框架可作为生育诊所中自动化精子分析的实用工具，兼具高性能和可解释性。

Abstract: Male infertility is a major cause of couple infertility, often linked to abnormal sperm morphology. While deep learning models offer automated analysis, most lack interpretability, limiting their clinical adoption. This study proposes an attention-guided deep learning framework for sperm morphology classification. We combine a pretrained EfficientNet-B0 with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to focus on key areas of the sperm head, improving both accuracy and interpretability. Evaluated on the SMIDS and HuSHem public datasets, our model achieves accuracies of 90.2% and 93.9% (macro F1 scores of 0.913 and 0.948), outperforming SimpleCNN and standard EfficientNet-B0. Furthermore, we use Grad-CAM++ visualizations to highlight features influencing the model's decisions. The results demonstrate that this accurate and transparent framework is a practical tool for automated sperm analysis in fertility clinics.

</details>


### [345] [Context-Aware Hierarchical Bayesian Modeling of IVF Laboratory Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20459)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Pia Saldeen,Lars Johansson,Thomas Ebner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用高分辨率实验室环境数据（55种时间感知特征）预测IVF妊娠率的方法，在亚洲诊所将预测误差降至1.27%，并通过分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型将知识迁移至北欧诊所，R²达0.86，误差降低64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IVF妊娠率模型主要使用患者层面变量，而高分辨率实验室环境数据（如培养箱微环境动态）未被充分利用，作者认为这是一个被忽视的机会。

Method: 1) 从原始传感器数据中工程化55种上下文感知的时间特征（滚动热稳定性、温湿度联合达标率、峰值应激时长、应激后恢复速度等）；2) 训练分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型，通过部分池化（partial pooling）跨诊所共享环境效应，同时保留各诊所的基线差异。

Result: 亚洲诊所61周数据上，预测误差从原始平均值的3-5%降至1.27%；北欧诊所留出数据上，R²=0.86，35-39岁年龄组误差较朴素基线降低64%。

Conclusion: 结构化的环境监测包含临床上有意义且可迁移的信号，跨诊所的知识迁移是可行的，环境动态特征比原始传感器平均值更具预测价值。

Abstract: IVF pregnancy rates are routinely modeled using patient-level variables, while high-resolution laboratory environmental data remain underutilized. We show that this is a missed opportunity. Rather than relying on raw sensor averages, we engineer 55 context-aware temporal features, including rolling thermal stability, simultaneous temperature-humidity adherence, peak stress duration, and post-stress recovery speed, that capture the dynamics of incubator microenvironments. On 61 weeks of data from an Asian IVF clinic, these features reduce cross-validated prediction error to 1.27%, compared to 3-5% for raw averages. We then train a hierarchical Bayesian Beta regression model that shares environmental effects across an Asian and a Northern European clinic via partial pooling, while preserving site-specific baselines. On held-out data from the Northern European clinic, the model achieves R2 = 0.86 and a 64% error reduction for the 35-39 age group over a naive baseline, demonstrating that structured environmental monitoring contains clinically meaningful, transferable signal.

</details>


### [346] [What Do Safety-Aligned LLMs Learn From Mixed Compliance Demonstrations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20508)
*Sihui Dai,Mann Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了上下文演示如何影响语言模型的 harmful compliance，发现良性演示与有害演示不可互换，其对 harmful compliance 的影响因模型而异；偏好优化是关键训练阶段，演示顺序存在近因偏差，且不同模型在拒绝时与上下文学习的交互方式不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明上下文演示可以越狱语言模型，但尚不清楚模型如何解释不同类型的 compliance 演示。本文旨在系统性地探究演示内容组合如何驱动 harmful compliance。

Method: 将良性 compliance 演示（无害请求+有用响应）与有害 compliance 演示（有害请求+有用响应）混合，提出三种关于演示组合如何驱动 harmful compliance 的假设，并在四个模型上进行测试。

Result: 1) 良性与有害演示不可互换，良性演示的影响因模型而异（有时减少、有时增加 harmful compliance）；2) 偏好优化是防止良性演示增加 harmful compliance 的关键训练阶段；3) 演示顺序存在强烈的近因偏差；4) 模型在拒绝时与上下文学习的交互方式不同：有些模型即使拒绝也采用演示的格式，有些则在拒绝时覆盖所有上下文信号。

Conclusion: 本文超越了"演示可以越狱"的展示，进一步刻画了其工作机制：模型从 compliance 演示中提取什么取决于演示内容、顺序和训练方法。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that in-context demonstrations can jailbreak language models, but it remains unclear how models interpret different types of compliance demonstrations. We study this by mixing benign compliance demonstrations (non-harmful request, helpful response) with harmful compliance demonstrations (harmful request, helpful response) and testing three hypotheses about how demonstration composition drives harmful compliance. Across four models, we find that benign and harmful demonstrations are not interchangeable: benign demonstrations can either reduce or increase harmful compliance depending on the model. We further show that preference optimization is the critical training stage that prevents benign demonstrations from increasing harmful compliance, that demonstration ordering exhibits strong recency bias, and that models differ in how refusal interacts with in-context learning: some adopt demonstrated formatting even when refusing, while others override all in-context signals upon refusal. Taken together, this work moves beyond showing that demonstration-based jailbreaking works to characterizing how it works: what models extract from compliance demonstrations depends on demonstration content, ordering, and training methodology.

</details>


### [347] [Multi-LCB: Extending LiveCodeBench to Multiple Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20517)
*Maria Ivanova,Pavel Zadorozhny,Rodion Levichev,Ivan Petrov,Adamenko Pavel,Ivan Lopatin,Alexey Kutalev,Dmitrii Babaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Multi-LCB扩展了LiveCodeBench基准测试，将其从仅支持Python扩展到涵盖12种编程语言，用于评估LLM的跨语言代码生成能力。对24个LLM的评估发现了Python过拟合、特定语言污染以及多语言性能差异等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiveCodeBench（LCB）虽然成为LLM代码生成任务的主流基准，但仅限于Python语言评估，无法反映LLM在真实软件工程中所需的多种编程语言泛化能力。

Method: 将LCB数据集中的Python任务转换为其他11种编程语言的等价任务，同时保留LCB的污染控制和评估协议，并与原始LCB格式完全兼容以自动追踪未来更新。

Result: 对24个指令和推理LLM的评估揭示了Python过拟合现象、特定语言的数据污染问题，以及多语言性能之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: Multi-LCB作为一个严格的多编程语言代码评估新基准，直接解决了LCB的主要局限性，揭示了当前LLM在跨语言代码生成方面的关键不足。

Abstract: LiveCodeBench (LCB) has recently become a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on code-generation tasks. By curating competitive programming problems, constantly adding fresh problems to the set, and filtering them by release dates, LCB provides contamination-aware evaluation and offers a holistic view of coding capability. However, LCB remains restricted to Python, leaving open the question of whether LLMs can generalize across the diverse programming languages required in real-world software engineering.
  We introduce Multi-LCB, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs across twelve programming languages, including Python. Multi-LCB transforms Python tasks from the LCB dataset into equivalent tasks in other languages while preserving LCB's contamination controls and evaluation protocol. Because it is fully compatible with the original LCB format, Multi-LCB will automatically track future LCB updates, enabling systematic assessment of cross-language code generation competence and requiring models to sustain performance well beyond Python.
  We evaluated 24 LLMs for instruction and reasoning on Multi-LCB, uncovering evidence of Python overfitting, language-specific contamination, and substantial disparities in multilingual performance. Our results establish Multi-LCB as a rigorous new benchmark for multi-programming-language code evaluation, directly addressing LCB's primary limitation and exposing critical gaps in current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [348] [FlowEdit: Associative Memory for Lifelong Pronunciation Adaptation in Flow-Matching TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20518)
*Harshit Singh,Ayush Pratap Singh,Nityanand Mathur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowEdit是一种针对冻结流匹配TTS系统的终身适配框架，通过在文本嵌入空间学习token级扰动来纠正发音错误，无需权重更新，使用现代Hopfield网络作为内容可寻址的情景记忆存储修正，在312个多语言专有名词上将音素错误率相对降低92.7%，修正时间约15秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配文本到语音系统在零样本场景下表现优异，但部署后静态不变，对词汇表外的专有名词存在发音错误，且需要重新训练才能修复，因此需要一种无需重训练即可持续修正发音的方法。

Method: FlowEdit在文本嵌入空间中优化token级扰动作为潜在条件编辑，通过现代Hopfield网络作为内容可寻址情景记忆存储修正，推理时通过带相似度门控的软注意力检索修正，支持模糊形态匹配。

Result: 在包含18个语系的312个多语言专有名词基准上，FlowEdit将目标词音素错误率相对零样本基线降低92.7%，同时保持通用语音质量不变，单GPU修正时间约15秒。

Conclusion: FlowEdit实现了对冻结流匹配TTS系统的终身发音修正适配，无需权重更新或重训练，高效且不影响整体语音质量，为TTS系统的持续改进提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Flow-matching text-to-speech systems achieve remarkable zero-shot quality but remain static after deployment: pronunciation errors on out-of-vocabulary proper nouns persist unless the model is retrained. We introduce FlowEdit, a life-long adaptation framework for frozen flow-matching TTS that learns pronunciation corrections as latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. When corrective feedback is provided, FlowEdit optimizes a token-level perturbation in the text embedding space, then stores the correction in a Modern Hopfield Network serving as content-addressable episodic memory. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [349] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSWIP提出了一种用于DeepProbLog程序的单世界反事实语义，通过神经物化将神经谓词转化为普通ProbLog选择，利用单世界干预程序（SWIP）和加权模型计数（WMC）实现精确反事实推理，相比深度孪生方法获得2.14倍加速，并揭示了神经校准退化对因果估计的偏差影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经符号系统（如DeepProbLog）的推理是关联性的，缺乏因果语义支持反事实推理。反事实推理对于干预和证据需要因果语义，现有方法存在计算开销大或精度不足的问题。

Method: 提出DeepSWIP单世界反事实语义：1）通过神经物化将固定上下文的神经谓词约简为普通ProbLog选择；2）应用单世界干预程序（SWIP）框架；3）在单个变换后的程序上通过加权模型计数（WMC）计算反事实。在有限基化和唯一支持模型假设下，相对于学习到的物化结构因果模型（FCM）是精确的。

Result: 在MPI3D数据集上，与DeepTwin构造方法在12,000个查询上验证了变换的正确性，并获得了2.14倍的推理加速（避免孪生网络的内生重复）。SUMO HOV实验表明神经校准退化会偏置plug-in估计，而正确限定的随机化策略AIPW估计器能消除总体均值和ATE估计的一阶偏差。

Conclusion: DeepSWIP为神经符号系统提供了精确且高效的反事实推理框架，标准商WMC形式的ProbLog条件式能够识别活跃的神经概率，解释干预清洗、校准敏感性和稀有证据不稳定性等问题。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [350] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LedgerAgent，一种在推理时维护独立任务状态账本的方法，用于客服领域遵循策略的工具调用智能体，相比标准提示方法显著提升了pass^k指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准工具调用智能体中任务状态未独立表示，观察、工具返回和策略指令都混在提示中，导致智能体每次决策时需从提示中重建状态。这种隐式状态管理造成两种常见故障：智能体可能检索到正确事实但后续基于过时/缺失/错误信息做决策；语法正确的工具调用可能违反依赖于当前任务状态的域策略。

Method: LedgerAgent在推理时维护一个独立的账本来记录观察到的任务状态，并将状态渲染到提示中。在执行改变环境的工具调用前，使用该账本检查依赖状态的策略约束，从而阻止策略违规。

Result: 在四个客服域和混合的开闭权重模型上，LedgerAgent相比标准基于提示的工具调用方法提升了平均pass^k指标，在更严格的多轮一致性指标下提升最大。

Conclusion: 显式维护任务状态账本并用于策略约束检查，能有效提升工具调用智能体在客服领域的策略遵循能力和一致性表现。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [351] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将DAAM框架适配到语音扩散模型领域，提出了跨注意力归因方法，用于分析风格化文本到语音系统中自然语言描述如何影响声学输出。通过对CapSpeech-TTS中3,600个（风格描述、文本转录）组合的分析，发现风格令牌时间方差低于内容/功能令牌，风格注意力与F0和能量相关，风格条件作用在早期步骤和深层达到峰值，注意力熵在第17层达到最小与风格重要性峰值共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风格化文本到语音系统使用自然语言控制语音特征，但单个词语如何影响声学输出仍不清楚，理解这一点对于诊断故障模式和改进可控性至关重要。

Method: 提出跨注意力归因方法，将DAAM框架首次适配到语音扩散模型领域，应用于CapSpeech-TTS。提取每个令牌在25层和24个ODE步骤上的热力图，分析120个风格描述条件生成30个文本转录的3,600种组合。

Result: (1) 风格令牌的时间方差低于内容/功能令牌，证实全局条件作用；(2) 风格注意力与F0和能量相关；(3) 风格条件作用在早期步骤和深层达到峰值；(4) 注意力熵在第17层达到最小，与风格重要性峰值共存，表明在最具风格关键性的阶段网络选择性最大。

Conclusion: 这是首次研究自然语言如何影响语音扩散模型中跨注意力机制的工作，揭示了风格令牌在扩散过程中的作用模式，为理解和改进风格化TTS系统提供了新的分析视角。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [352] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合专家模型（MoE）在分布偏移下的校准行为，发现硬路由模型中专家级校准足以保证整体模型校准，但软路由模型则不足。为此提出对抗性重加权方法，通过惩罚分布偏移下的路由聚合校准误差，提升了平均和困难子集上的准确率-校准权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作表明在个体预测器层面强制校准可以提升集成模型和MoE的准确率与校准性能，但校准在何种条件下对MoE有效尚不清楚。本文旨在研究MoE模型在分布偏移下的行为，特别是路由机制与专家级校准之间的交互作用。

Method: 首先理论分析硬路由和软路由MoE模型在分布偏移下的校准性质，证明硬路由模型中专家校准足以保证整体校准，而软路由模型则不足。然后提出对抗性重加权方法，通过优化一个对抗性权重分布来惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合的校准误差，从而改善准确率-校准权衡。

Result: 硬路由MoE下专家级校准时整体模型在广泛分布偏移类中保持校准；软路由MoE下专家校准不足以保证整体校准。提出的对抗性重加权方法在多种模型类别、预测任务和分布偏移场景下，均能提升平均和困难数据子集上的准确率-校准权衡。

Conclusion: 硬路由和软路由MoE在分布偏移下的校准行为存在本质差异，针对软路由模型设计的对抗性重加权方法能有效改善校准性能，为理解和提升MoE模型的可靠性提供了理论依据和实用工具。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [353] [LLM-Mediated Human-AI Interaction in Search and Rescue: Impact of Expertise on Attentional Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19514)
*Elahe Oveisi,Hemanth Manjunatha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在搜索与救援模拟环境中，LLM（大语言模型）作为AI助手对人类认知和行为的影响。实验设置了两种LLM引导条件和一种无LLM基线条件，通过任务表现、眼动追踪和规划行为等多层面分析发现：LLM引导提升了任务效率（每步获得的奖励和救援目标更多），但并未增加总救援数量；眼动数据显示存在注意力引导的权衡，视觉资源向聊天界面转移且瞳孔大小变异性增加；专家和新手表现出不同的交互模式——新手倾向于被动依赖AI，而专家则通过持续的“验证循环”保持对环境的扫描。研究结论指出，LLM辅助团队协作的效果取决于操作者能否将AI建议与实际情况交叉验证以维持情境意识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在复杂任务中提供实时、上下文感知的指导越来越普遍，理解AI辅助如何塑造人类认知和行为变得至关重要。特别是，AI辅助可能引入认知负荷并影响注意力分配、规划以及与任务环境的交互，这些效应在不同专业水平之间存在差异。现有研究对LLM引导下的人类-AI协作机制缺乏深入理解。

Method: 在模拟搜索与救援（SAR）环境中进行了受控实验，比较了两种LLM（大语言模型）引导条件和一种无LLM基线条件下的人类表现。采用多层面分析：任务层面（奖励、救援数、每步效率）、眼动追踪（注意力分配、瞳孔大小变化）和规划行为分析。通过专家与新手的对比来考察专业水平的调节效应。

Result: LLM引导提升了任务效率（更高的奖励和每步救援数），但并未增加总救援数量。眼动数据揭示了注意力引导的权衡：视觉资源向聊天界面转移，同时瞳孔大小变异性增加。专业水平调节了这些效应：新手表现出对AI的被动依赖，而专家则维持“验证循环”——通过持续的环境扫描来交叉验证AI建议。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的团队协作效果取决于操作者能否将AI建议与实际情况交叉验证以维持情境意识。这提示在设计人类-AI协作系统时，需要支持用户的验证行为而非仅提供指导，同时需考虑不同专业水平用户的差异化需求。

Abstract: Human-AI teaming (HAT) increasingly involves AI systems that provide real-time, context-aware guidance in complex tasks. While such systems can improve performance, their effectiveness depends on how they shape human cognition and behavior. In particular, AI assistance can introduce cognitive demands and influence attention, planning, and interaction with the task environment, with effects that can vary across levels of expertise. This work investigates these mechanisms in a simulated search and rescue (SAR) environment. We compare human performance under two LLM (Large Language Model)-guided conditions and a no-LLM baseline, and analyze interaction at multiple levels, including task performance, eye-tracking measures, and planning behavior. Eye tracking provides fine-grained insight into attention allocation and interaction with AI guidance, while behavioral measures capture how users structure and adapt their decisions over time. Results indicate that LLM guidance enhanced task efficiency (higher rewards and victims-per-step) but did not increase total victims saved. Eye-tracking data revealed an attention-guidance trade-off, with visual resources shifting to the chat interface alongside increased pupil size variability. Expertise moderated this effect: novices exhibited passive AI reliance, whereas experts maintained a "verification loop" through persistent environmental scanning. These findings suggest that LLM-mediated teaming efficacy depends on the operator's ability to cross-reference AI guidance with ground truth to maintain situational awareness.

</details>


### [354] [Code as Anchor, Memory and Metaphor as Support: Learner Experiences with Multi-View Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19570)
*Naaz Sibia,Jessica Wen,Amber Richardson,Yashika Jain,Khushi Malik,Bogdan Simion,Carolina Nobre,Angela Zavaleta Bernuy,Andrew Petersen,Michael Liut*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对19名已完成CS1和CS2的本科生进行眼动追踪、出声思维和反思性访谈，发现学生在使用多表征编程可视化工具时，近一半时间聚焦在代码上。学生选择性参与可视化工具受到三个因素影响：能动性（控制认知努力）、表征适配性（同一设计在不同情境下的感受差异）和合法性（将隐喻性脚手架视为不够严谨）。研究表明，计算教育中的多表征工具需同时关注认知设计、情感因素和社会因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序可视化工具被广泛用于支持编程初学者，但学生经常忽视或抗拒这些精心设计的可视化脚手架。尽管多外部表征(MER)研究提供了协调视图的认知设计原则，但影响学习者参与可用表征的因素尚不明确。

Method: 对19名已完成CS1和CS2的本科生进行被试内研究，使用包含同步代码、内存和隐喻视图的多表征探测工具及Python Tutor，通过出声思维任务、反思性访谈和基于网络摄像头的眼动追踪收集数据。

Result: 眼动分析显示，尽管有可视化脚手架，学生近一半时间聚焦在代码上。无先前经验的学生更严重地锚定在代码上，且极少参与隐喻视图。访谈识别出三个影响选择性参与的因素：能动性（学生寻求控制认知努力而非简单减少努力）、表征适配性（相同设计在不同情境下感觉有帮助或压倒性）、合法性（部分学生认为隐喻脚手架过于幼稚，不适合大学水平学习）。

Conclusion: 计算教育中的多表征工具需要在认知设计之外关注情感和社会因素。实践建议包括：将可视化定位为验证工具、提供可切换的抽象层级、以及框架工具以传达学科合法性。这些发现有助于解释为何认知上合理的可视化工具可能无法吸引它们旨在帮助的学生。

Abstract: Program visualizations are widely used to support novice programmers, yet students often ignore or resist well-designed visual scaffolds. Research on multiple external representations (MERs) offers cognitive design principles for coordinating views, but less is known about what shapes learners' engagement with available representations.
  We conducted a within-subjects study with 19 undergraduates who had completed CS1 and CS2. Students completed think-aloud tasks, reflective interviews, and webcam-based gaze tracking while using a multi-representational probe with synchronized code, memory, and metaphor views, and Python Tutor, across scope, while loops, and linked lists.
  Gaze analysis showed that students spent nearly half their time focused on code despite available visual scaffolds. Students without prior experience anchored even more heavily in code and engaged minimally with metaphor views. Interviews identified three factors shaping selective engagement: agency, as students sought control over cognitive effort rather than simply having it reduced; representational fit, as identical designs differed in whether they felt helpful or overwhelming; and legitimacy, as some students avoided metaphorical scaffolds they perceived as childish or insufficiently rigorous for university-level work.
  These findings suggest that multi-representational tools in computing education require attention to affective and social factors alongside cognitive design. Practical considerations include positioning visualizations as verification instruments, offering toggleable abstraction levels, and framing tools to signal disciplinary legitimacy. More broadly, the themes help explain why cognitively sound visualization tools may fail to engage the students they are designed to help.

</details>


### [355] [Building Drift: Documenting On-Site Construction Adaptations Across Material Lifecycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19609)
*Ritik Batra,Martin Tamke,Tom Svilans,Jan Hüls,Amritansh Kwatra,Steven J. Jackson,Thijs Roumen,Mette Ramsgaard Thomsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"建筑漂移"概念，描述回收材料建筑中物理状态与数字模型之间的集体偏差，通过ReShelter案例构建了五类漂移的分类法，并开发了Pentimento工具，利用视频文档和3D高斯泼溅技术在时空和语义上表征现场 adaptations，支持回收材料的循环利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环经济中回收材料的使用引入不可预测性，需要现场即兴调整，但缺乏文档记录导致后续合作者、评估者和继承者无法获取必要信息，难以实现材料的规模化再利用和跨生命周期管理。

Method: 通过ReShelter（森林中建造的回收木材展馆）案例研究，归纳出建筑漂移的五类分类法；在此基础上开发Pentimento工具，结合视频文档和3D高斯泼溅技术，对现场adaptations进行空间、时间和语义层面的表征。

Result: 构建了建筑漂移分类法：场地照料、适配搜寻、材料解读、测量标记和社区协调；Pentimento工具使各利益相关方能够导航材料历史，降低材料再利用的障碍。

Conclusion: 该研究为支持回收材料建筑所必需的现场即兴创作开辟了计算工具路径，有助于实现更可持续的回收、修复和再利用循环。

Abstract: In a circular economy for construction, reclaimed materials carry prior lives of use and go on to have post-lives in future buildings. Yet working with such materials introduces unpredictability that requires on-site improvisation, making their reuse challenging to document and scale across building lifetimes. Without documentation, the on-site adaptations that make construction with reclaimed materials possible leave collaborators, evaluators, and inheritors without the information they need to continue, assess, and reuse materials. We call the collective deviation of the physical state from the digital model through these adaptations "building drift." Through a case study, ReShelter, a reclaimed timber pavilion constructed in the forest, we develop a taxonomy for building drift that characterizes the collective deviation across building lifetimes: Tending the Site, Foraging for Fit, Interpreting the Material, Marking Measurements, and Coordinating Across Communities.
  To put our taxonomy for building drift into practice, we present Pentimento, a documentation tool that leverages video documentation and 3D Gaussian Splatting to spatially, temporally, and semantically represent on-site adaptations in relation to the designed model. Pentimento enables each stakeholder to navigate material histories in ways that reduce barriers to material reuse. Together, these contributions open pathways towards computational tools that support the on-site improvisation essential to construction with reclaimed materials, enabling more sustainable cycles of recovery, repair, and reuse.

</details>


### [356] [Syndesmoscope: The Power of Invariant Plots\\Linked to Traditional Network Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19689)
*Matt Oddo,Indira Sowy,Stephen Kobourov,Tamara Munzner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出Syndesmoscope系统，通过并列展示力导向图、密集-稀疏梯度、测地离心率、谱二分和kSnakes（新型密度分解不变图）等多视图，结合跳跃式关联高亮和跳房子式拓扑遍历交互，实现对网络拓扑不变模式的探索。在72个网络上的使用场景验证了多视图协作能揭示单一视图无法呈现的网络模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络可视化（节点-链接图、邻接矩阵）的视觉模式严重依赖布局或排序算法，而基于图论的不变图（invariant plots）能稳定呈现相同拓扑的相同视觉模式，但研究者对其探索不足且未将其集成到可视化系统中。

Method: 1) 提出Syndesmoscope系统，并列力导向视图、密集-稀疏梯度视图、测地离心率视图、谱二分视图四个面板；2) 引入kSnakes不变图，基于密度分解（k-core分解的变体）；3) 设计leapfrogging（跨视图关联高亮）和hopscotching（基于跳数的拓扑遍历与选择扩展）两种交互范式。

Result: 在包含72个多样网络的语料库上的使用场景表明，多视图协作与跨视图交互能揭示单一视图无法访问的网络模式。

Conclusion: 不变图与多视图联动交互是网络探索的有效方法，Syndesmoscope证明了集成不变图到可视化系统中的可行性。

Abstract: Traditional network representations, such as node-link views and adjacency matrices, can show dramatically different visual patterns, depending on the underlying layout or seriation algorithm. In contrast, invariant plots consistently surface the same visual pattern for the same input topology; yet researchers have underexplored them and have not integrated them into visualization systems. We present Syndesmoscope, an interactive system for network exploration that juxtaposes multiple views of the same network. Panes show a familiar a force-directed view alongside three panes with interpretable geometric layouts based on graph-theoretic properties: dense-sparse gradient, geodesic eccentricity, and spectral bisection. As a secondary contribution, we introduce kSnakes, a new invariant plot based on density decomposition. Syndesmoscope supports two key interactions: leapfrogging, or linked highlighting between different and interpretable visual patterns; and hopscotching, or hop-based traversal that extends data selections through the underlying topology. Through usage scenarios across a corpus of 72 diverse networks, we demonstrate how these interactions reveal network patterns inaccessible through any single view alone. Live demo available at https://syndesmoscope.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [357] [Vibe Coding for Visualization Implementation: An Empirical Study of Practices and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19703)
*Zhengyu Sun,Xiaolin Wen,Fengjie Wang,Can Liu,Yi Lai,Christophe Hurter,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过16名参与者的实证研究，考察了用户如何使用AI驱动的"vibe coding"工具实现数据可视化，发现用户在提示、评估和迭代过程中采用了多样化实践，并面临诸多挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据可视化对数据分析和沟通至关重要，但创建富有表现力的可视化仍然劳动密集。虽然AI驱动的"vibe coding"工具通过自然语言交互降低了门槛，但可视化实现需要用户意图与视觉表示之间的精确对齐，这与通用软件开发实践可能有所不同，因此需要系统研究用户如何使用这些工具进行可视化实现。

Method: 开展了包含16名不同专业水平参与者的实证研究，参与者完成两个可视化任务并进行半结构化访谈。

Result: 研究刻画了用户在提示、评估和迭代过程中采用的多样化实践，并揭示了他们在整个过程中遇到的挑战。

Conclusion: 用户在使用vibe coding工具进行可视化实现时存在多样化实践和独特挑战，表明可视化实现与通用软件开发在使用AI工具方面存在差异，需要针对性的支持。

Abstract: Data visualization is essential for data analysis and communication, yet creating expressive visualizations remains labor-intensive. Recent AI-driven ``vibe coding'' tools enable users to generate visualizations through natural language interaction, lowering the barrier to entry. However, visualization implementation requires precise alignment between user intent and visual representation, which may differ from general software development practices. We present an empirical study with 16 participants of varying expertise to examine how users employ vibe coding tools for visualization implementation. Participants completed two visualization tasks and a semi-structured interview. Our findings characterize the diverse practices users adopt across prompting, evaluation, and iteration, and surface the challenges they encounter throughout the process.

</details>


### [358] [Designing for Interconnected Islamic Learning: A Qualitative Study of Muslim Women's Experiences with Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19745)
*Ishrat Jahan Easha,Nabil Mosharraf Hossain,Araf Mohammad Mahbub,Fairoze Bint Abu Hassan,Zunaid Aslam,Yemin Sajid,Riasat Islam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过5次半结构化访谈，探讨穆斯林女性在数字阅读古兰经、圣训和先知传记时面临的信息分离问题，提出"分层情境性"概念，强调情境扩展必须在解释问责、灵修流畅性和跨设备连续性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊斯兰学习通常需要同时阅读古兰经、圣训和先知传记，但现有数字工具将这些资源分散在不同的应用、界面和搜索路径中，破坏了学习的连贯性和情境性。

Method: 从在线伊斯兰学习社区招募5名穆斯林女性进行半结构化访谈，通过性别化数字宗教、认知信任和无缝学习等理论视角分析访谈数据，提取五个主题。

Result: 识别出五个主题：情境理解、真实性、界面杂乱、学习模式和引导功能。参与者希望阅读时能获得跨文本情境，但前提是情境扩展必须可信、可选且不干扰阅读流程。

Conclusion: 提出"分层情境性"作为人机交互领域的新概念框架：数字宗教文本中的情境扩展需要平衡解释问责、灵修流畅性以及跨设备和学习强度的连续性。

Abstract: Islamic learning often depends on reading the Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah together, yet digital tools typically separate these sources across apps, screens, and search pathways. We examine this as a human-computer interaction problem through five semi-structured interviews with Muslim women recruited from an online Islamic learning community. Participants described a recurring tension: they wanted Qur'an-Hadith-Seerah context at the point of reading, but only when contextual expansion remained trustworthy, optional, and did not interrupt reading. Interpreting the interviews through gendered digital religion, epistemic trust, and seamless learning, we identify five themes concerning contextual understanding, authenticity, interface clutter, study modes, and guidance features. We introduce layered contextuality as an HCI account of this domain: contextual expansion must be balanced with interpretive accountability, devotional flow, and continuity across devices and study intensities.

</details>


### [359] [MemGUI-Agent: An End-to-End Long-Horizon Mobile GUI Agent with Proactive Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19926)
*Guangyi Liu,Gao Wu,Congxiao Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Liang Liu,Mading Li,Qi Zhang,Mengyan Wang,Liang Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MemGUI-Agent通过将上下文管理作为第一类动作（ConAct）解决了长时序移动GUI代理中ReAct式提示导致的提示爆炸和关键信息稀释问题，在8B参数规模上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于MLLM的移动GUI代理在短时任务上表现良好，但在需要跨多步和跨应用保留中间事实的长时序任务中不可靠，原因是ReAct风格的提示会被动累积每步记录，导致提示爆炸和跨应用关键事实被稀释。

Method: 提出MemGUI-Agent，核心是Context-as-Action (ConAct)机制，将上下文管理视为与UI动作同策略发出的第一类动作；维护三个结构化上下文字段：折叠动作历史、折叠UI状态和最近步骤记录；并构建了包含2,956条轨迹的MemGUI-3K数据集用于监督训练。

Result: 在MemGUI-Bench上达到8B开源数据最佳性能，并能泛化到分布外的MobileWorld基准测试。

Conclusion: 将上下文管理建模为可学习的动作能够有效解决长时序移动GUI代理中的上下文爆炸问题，且可跨模型规模迁移学习。

Abstract: MLLM-based mobile GUI agents have made substantial progress on short-horizon tasks, yet remain unreliable on long-horizon tasks that require retaining intermediate facts across many steps and app transitions. We attribute this limitation to ReAct-style prompting, which passively accumulates per-step records, leading to prompt explosion and dilution of critical cross-app facts. To address this, we introduce MemGUI-Agent, an end-to-end long-horizon mobile GUI agent with proactive context management. MemGUI-Agent is built on Context-as-Action (ConAct), which casts context management as first-class actions emitted by the same policy that selects UI actions. Instead of passively appending history, ConAct maintains three structured context fields: folded action history, folded UI state, and recent step record, preserving critical UI facts while keeping context compact.
